Escape sequences are never split, even if they are malformed or\n// truncated.\nfunc Tokenize(s string) []Token {\n\tvar tokens []Token\n\n\ttextStart := 0\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(s); {\n\t\tif s[i] != ESC {\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\traw, typ, next := parseEscape(s, i)\n\t\tif i > textStart {\n\t\t\ttext := s[textStart:i]\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: text, Text: text})\n\t\t}\n\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: typ, Raw: raw})\n\t\ti = next\n\t\ttextStart = next\n\t}\n\tif textStart < len(s) {\n\t\ttext := s[textStart:]\n\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: text, Text: text})\n\t}\n\n\treturn tokens\n}\n\n// parseEscape parses the escape sequence starting at position i and returns\n// its raw representation, its type and the position just past the sequence.\nfunc parseEscape(s string, i int) (string, TokenType, int) {\n\t// A lone escape character at the end of the string.\n\tif i+1 >= len(s) {\n\t\treturn s[i:], TokenSGR, len(s)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch s[i+1] {\n\tcase '[':\n\t\tj := i + 2\n\t\t// Consume parameter and intermediate bytes.\n\t\tfor j < len(s) && s[j] >= 0x20 && s[j] <= 0x3f {\n\t\t\tj++\n\t\t}\n\t\t// A final byte is required to terminate the sequence.\n\t\tif j >= len(s) || s[j] < 0x40 || s[j] > 0x7e {\n\t\t\treturn s[i:], TokenSGR, len(s)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\traw := s[i : j+1]\n\t\tif s[j] == 'm' && isResetSGR(s[i+2:j]) {\n\t\t\treturn raw, TokenReset, j + 1\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn raw, TokenSGR, j + 1\n\n\tcase ']':\n\t\tj := i + 2\n\t\tfor j < len(s) {\n\t\t\tif s[j] == BEL {\n\t\t\t\treturn s[i : j+1], oscType(s[i+2 : j]), j + 1\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif s[j] == ESC && j+1 < len(s) && s[j+1] == '\\\\' {\n\t\t\t\treturn s[i : j+2], oscType(s[i+2 : j]), j + 2\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tj++\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Unterminated OSC sequence: consume the rest of the string.\n\t\treturn s[i:], TokenSGR, len(s)\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\t// Two or three byte escape sequences, e.g. ESC c or ESC ( 0. Consume\n\t\t// any intermediate bytes followed by a final byte.\n\t\tj := i + 1\n\t\tfor j < len(s) && s[j] >= 0x20 && s[j] <= 0x2f {\n\t\t\tj++\n\t\t}\n\t\tif j < len(s) && s[j] >= 0x30 && s[j] <= 0x7e {\n\t\t\treturn s[i : j+1], TokenSGR, j + 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tif j == i+1 && j < len(s) {\n\t\t\treturn s[i : i+2], TokenSGR, i + 2\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn s[i:j], TokenSGR, j\n\t}\n}\n\n// oscType returns the token type for the given OSC payload.\nfunc oscType(payload string) TokenType {\n\tif !strings.HasPrefix(payload, \"8;\") {\n\t\treturn TokenSGR\n\t}\n\trest := payload[2:]\n\tidx := strings.IndexByte(rest, ';')\n\tif idx < 0 {\n\t\treturn TokenSGR\n\t}\n\tif rest[idx+1:] == \"\" {\n\t\treturn TokenHyperlinkClose\n\t}\n\treturn TokenHyperlinkOpen\n}\n\n// isResetSGR reports whether the given SGR parameter string is a reset. An\n// empty parameter string, an empty parameter or any parameter that parses to\n// zero are all considered a reset.\nfunc isResetSGR(params string) bool {\n\tif params == \"\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tfor _, p := range strings.Split(params, \";\") {\n\t\tif p == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t\tif n, err := strconv.Atoi(p); err == nil && n == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// StripANSI removes all ANSI escape sequences from s.\nfunc StripANSI(s string) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.Grow(len(s))\n\tfor _, t := range Tokenize(s) {\n\t\tif t.Type == TokenText {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(t.Text)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ANSIWidth returns the visible width of s, ignoring any ANSI escape\n// sequences. Wide runes count as two cells and zero-width runes as zero.\nfunc ANSIWidth(s string) int {\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(StripANSI(s))\n}\n\n// HasANSI reports whether s contains any ANSI escape sequences.\nfunc HasANSI(s string) bool {\n\tfor _, t := range Tokenize(s) {\n\t\tif t.Type != TokenText {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n", "structuredPatch": [{"oldStart": 39, "oldLines": 6, "newStart": 39, "newLines": 23, "lines": ["   TokenHyperlinkClose", " )", " ", "+// String returns the name of the token type.", "+func (t TokenType) String() string {", "+  switch t {", "+  case TokenText:", "+    return \"Text\"", "+  case TokenSGR:", "+    return \"SGR\"", "+  case TokenReset:", "+    return \"Reset\"", "+  case TokenHyperlinkOpen:", "+    return \"HyperlinkOpen\"", "+  case TokenHyperlinkClose:", "+    return \"HyperlinkClose\"", "+  }", "+  return \"Unknown\"", "+}", "+", " // Token is a single token of an ANSI escaped string.", " type Token struct {", "   // Type is the type of the token."]}], "userModified": false, "replaceAll": false}, "origin": null}}
